# Changelog

# 📦 8.1

## 📰 General changes and notes

Summary of changes here!

<details open>
<summary>Github Commits :octocat:</summary>
<blockquote>

- chore: changelogs
- chore: fix changelog link
- fix: change to direct call in ban scripts
- fix: BE not just entity
- Improve Turkish Quests translations (#4321)
- * Add Turkish quest translation
- * Rework Turkish localization and fix QA issues
- * Fix Forbidden Arcanus and update SNBT files
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations
- * fix: improve Turkish FTB Quests translations
- * fix: clean up Pylons chapter title
- * Update Turkish quest translations
- * fix: sync Turkish quest translations
- * fix: align Turkish quest translations with official terminology
- * fix: align Turkish quest translations with mod localizations
- * fix: align Deeper and Darker quests with Turkish localization
- Fix #4322: Some quest typos (#4323)
- Updated PT_BR Localizations (#4326)
- * Updated PT_BR Localizations
- * Misc Fix
- * Misc Fix
- Auroral backport and Create missing image (#4331)
- * Create missing image
- * Auroral Quest Backport
- It works!
- * Revert "Auroral Quest Backport"
- This reverts commit 79f3df7e56fe803dcfa4a36cfce2d9a59711e025.
- * Auroral Backport
- Fixed Chapter Lang
- Add files via upload (#4336)
- Add ATM10A on README modpack list (#4341)
- Disable new silver ore from Mekanism: More Machine (#4344)
- Prevent infinite electrode durability via Mahou Tsukai Strengthening (#4349)
- Prevent Potential ATM Ore Progression Bypasses Through Neo Vitae (#4350)
- Glowing Hellshelf Quest Changes (#4354)
- * Glowing Hellshelf Quest Changes
- Adjusted all quests involving Glowing Hellshelves that use 12 of them for certain infusions to use 13 of them instead. See https://github.com/AllTheMods/ATM-10-a/issues/40 for why I made this decision.
- Additionally: Changes the Spawner Upgrade Rune text to match the colors of the respective Rarity Material used to craft them.
- And fixes an error I made with the Tome of Extraction's quest.
- * Fix Steam Engine quest text for ru_ru
- chore: bcc
- Some Quick Quest Changes (#4359)
- - Removed Copper Alloy Bee's quest
- - Toned down the Petty Spiritus Gem requirement for the Hellfire Forge
- Prevent Feral Flare Invisible Lights from blocking Enchanting (#4360)
- fix(quests): auroral missing items
- fix(ad_astra): compat recipes
- fix(ad_astra): more compat recipes
- chore: modlist</blockquote>

</details>
